### 596. assistant (2026-06-20T07:34:55.741Z)

#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Asserts the two live hosts (Cloudflare + droplet) serve BYTE-IDENTICAL content.
# Catches deploy drift the unit/integration tests can't: they verify the code works on
# each host, NOT that the two live URLs are in sync. (.gitattributes keeps line endings
# identical so a stale scp can't masquerade as a "difference".)
#
#   CF_URL=https://khelsutra.guru VM_URL=http://168.144.126.176:8787 scripts/parity-check.sh
set -euo pipefail
CF="${CF_URL:-https://khelsutra.guru}"
VM="${VM_URL:-http://168.144.126.176:8787}"
UA="Mozilla/5.0 (compatible; khelsutra-parity)"
fail=0
for p in "" capture; do
  a=$(curl -fsS -A "$UA" --max-time 25 "$CF/$p?x=$RANDOM" | md5sum | cut -d' ' -f1)
  b=$(curl -fsS -A "$UA" --max-time 25 "$VM/$p?x=$RANDOM" | md5sum | cut -d' ' -f1)
  if [ "$a" = "$b" ]; then echo "OK    /$p  $a"; else echo "DRIFT /$p  cf=$a  vm=$b"; fail=1; fi
done
if [ "$fail" = 0 ]; then echo "PARITY: in sync"; else
  echo "PARITY: DRIFT — re-sync the droplet from git (LF):"
  echo "  git -C <repo> archive HEAD:site | ssh root@<vm> tar -x -C /srv/khelsutra-site/site"
  exit 1
fi

3. Files and Code Sections (khelsutra repo = `C:\Users\avidu\Projects\khelsutra`, the brand repo `avidullu/khelsutra`):
   - **`site/public/index.html`** — the landing page (segmented Request-Access form, "Khel ▸ Sutra" play-triangle logo, today-positioning "Every rally, indexed. The dead time, gone.", "Coming next to the alpha" section). Form posts JSON to `/api/request-access` with mailto fallback.
   - **`site/public/capture.html`** — printable 1-page capture checklist; served at `/capture`.
   - **`site/public/og.png`** — 1200×630 OG image (generated by `site/make_og.py`, Pillow + Windows fonts; writes to `public/og.png`).
   - **`site/src/index.js`** — the Cloudflare Worker. Key exported functions: `validate`, `toRow`, `isBot`, `handleRequest(request, store, notify)`, default `{ fetch }`. The fetch wires: `return handleRequest(request, d1Store(env.DB), cfEmailNotify(env.NOTIFY, env.NOTIFY_FROM, env.NOTIFY_TO));`. handleRequest: validate→honeypot→toRow→`await store.insertAccessRequest(r)`→`try { await notify?.notify(r); } catch {}`→`json({ok:true})`.
   - **`site/src/store.js`** — storage adapters. Exports `INSERT_SQL`, `insertArgs`, `d1Store(DB)` (uses `DB.prepare(INSERT_SQL).bind(...insertArgs(r)).run()`), `sqliteStore(db)` (uses `db.prepare(INSERT_SQL); stmt.run(...insertArgs(r))`).
   - **`site/src/notify.js`** — `buildSubmissionEmail(r)` (returns {subject, text}), `cfEmailNotify(binding, from, to)` (dynamic `import("cloudflare:email")` → `binding.send(new EmailMessage(from, to, raw))`), `noopNotify()`.
   - **`site/server.js`** — dependency-free Node VM host (node:http + node:sqlite). Serves `public/` + handles `POST /api/request-access` via `handleRequest(await toRequest(req), store, notify)` where `store = sqliteStore(db)`, `notify = noopNotify()`. Env: PORT (default 8080), DB_PATH. Uses `import.meta.dirname`.
   - **`site/src/index.test.js`** — 12 Cloudflare-logic tests + 3 notify tests (validate, isBot, toRow, handleRequest+store, d1Store, sqliteStore real round-trip, fetch routing, 405, buildSubmissionEmail, notify-called/best-effort, no-notify-on-bot/bad).
   - **`site/src/server.test.js`** — VM-host integration harness: spawns real `server.js` (free port + temp DB), round-trips via fetch, verifies SQLite persistence. 5 tests.
   - **`site/wrangler.toml`** — `name = "hidden-king-khelsutra"` (must match the git-connected Worker), `main = "src/index.js"`, `[assets] directory = "./public" binding = "ASSETS"`, `[[d1_databases]] binding = "DB" database_name = "khelsutra-access" database_id = "cd02c6bf-4bd6-4a33-a333-ced86e36b511"`, `[[send_email]] name = "NOTIFY" destination_address = "avi.dullu@gmail.com"`, `[vars] NOTIFY_FROM = "noreply@khelsutra.guru" NOTIFY_TO = "avi.dullu@gmail.com"`.
   - **`site/schema.sql`** — `access_requests` table (id, created_at, segment, name, email, city, org_name, courts, has_gpu, storage, message, consent, country, user_agent).
   - **`site/package.json`** — scripts: `"test": "node --test src/index.test.js src/server.test.js"`, `test:cf`, `test:vm`, `deploy`, `dev`, `og`.
   - **`.github/workflows/test.yml`** — runs `npm test` on push to master + PRs, Node 24, working-directory `site`.
   - **`site/scripts/droplet-smoke.sh`** — live scp+boot+round-trip against a VM (SSH_TARGET env). Executable.
   - **`site/scripts/parity-check.sh`** — asserts CF and VM serve byte-identical content (md5 of `/` and `/capture`). Executable.

4. Errors and fixes:
   - **wrangler.toml name mismatch:** git-connect attached to existing Worker `hidden-king-khelsutra` but wrangler.toml said `khelsutra-site` → deploys missed the live site (khelsutra.guru 404 on /api). Fixed by PR #5 renaming `name = "hidden-king-khelsutra"`. NO domain cutover needed (good case).
   - **Preview served wrong dir:** copied updated index.html to `artifacts/site_deploy/` but the preview server (port 8099) served `artifacts/landing/`. Fixed by copying to `artifacts/landing/index.html`.
   - **Screenshot tool flakiness:** preview_screenshot timed out after cross-page navigation; worked around with DOM checks (preview_eval/inspect) and curl.
   - **`node --test src/` aggregated to "tests 1":** directory mode showed misleading count; switched to explicit file list `node --test src/index.test.js src/server.test.js` → clear 17→20 count.
   - **`curl -w '%{content_type}'/'%{size_download}'` gave "(43) bad argument"** on the Windows curl build; worked around using `-i | head -1` and `-o /dev/null -w '%{http_code}'`.
   - **WebFetch 403 on khelsutra.guru** (Cloudflare bot-block) → used `curl` with a browser User-Agent.
   - **CRITICAL — line-ending drift (user-reported):** CF (LF, md5 1598a01a9810) vs droplet (CRLF, md5 649a5574b9e6); user said "verify for yourself that the two websites differ!! ... why did the test not catch this!" Diagnosis: droplet scp'd from Windows working copy (autocrlf→CRLF), CF from git (LF); content byte-identical after stripping CR. Fixed: normalized droplet to LF (now md5-match), added `.gitattributes` (force LF) + `site/scripts/parity-check.sh` (PR #10). Explained the tests check code/behavior per host, not byte-parity between live deployments, and CRLF/LF doesn't affect functionality or `id="af"` checks.

-> **⚠ Two env vars you MUST set, or the tunnel is dead-on-arrival.** With `edge_auth_enabled: true`, the engine (a) flips its bind to `0.0.0.0` and (b) keeps the DNS-rebinding **Host-header guard** at its loopback-only default — so it boots "healthy" but then **`400`s every request `cloudflared` forwards with `Host: api.khelsutra.guru`** (including the §6 `/api/health` check). The boot only *warns* about this (`EXPOSED_HOST_ALLOWLIST_LOOPBACK`), it does not stop. ### 673. user (2026-06-22T09:52:47.070Z)

=== _allowed_hosts_from_env: REPLACE or append the loopback default? ===
def _allowed_hosts_from_env(env: Optional[Dict[str, str]] = None) -> List[str]:
    """P0c: Host-header allowlist — a DNS-rebinding guard for the localhost appliance. A malicious
    web page can point a domain it controls at 127.0.0.1 and script requests to the local server;
    the browser sends ``Host: attacker.com``, which this rejects (the server only answers loopback
    Host headers by default). ``RALLY_ALLOWED_HOSTS`` (comma-separated) overrides; the default keeps
    Starlette's TestClient host ``testserver`` so the in-process suite isn't broken. Read from the
    ENV (not config.json) so importing ``backend.main`` does zero filesystem I/O; a routable/edge
    deploy sets its public host (or ``*`` when fronted by an auth gate that validates the host)."""
    src = os.environ if env is None else env
    raw = (src.get("RALLY_ALLOWED_HOSTS", "") or "").strip()
    hosts = [h.strip() for h in raw.split(",") if h.strip()]
    # IPv4 loopback (the default bind) + the TestClient host. NB: Starlette's TrustedHostMiddleware
    # matches on host.split(":")[0], which mangles a bracketed IPv6 Host ("[::1]:port"), so IPv6
    # loopback is NOT supported by this guard — access the appliance via 127.0.0.1/localhost.
    return hosts or ["localhost", "127.0.0.1", "testserver"]


# DNS-rebinding guard. Bound to localhost by default, so only loopback Host headers are honoured
# (a remote page can't read responses cross-origin AND its Host header is rejected here). www_redirect
# off so an unexpected ``www.`` host is rejected rather than 307-redirected.

+The request-access logic in `src/index.js` is runtime-agnostic: it runs both as the **Cloudflare
+Worker** (this README's focus; D1 + Email Routing) and on an **always-on VM host** (`server.js`;
+`node:sqlite`, no email yet). All storage/notify coupling lives in `src/store.js` / `src/notify.js`.
+See `docs/PORTABILITY.md`.
